src/utils/money.js:export function splitInstallments(totalCents, count, firstDue = new Date()) {
src/utils/money.js:  if (![1,2,3].includes(count)) throw new Error('count must be 1|2|3');
src/utils/money.js:  const base = Math.floor(totalCents / count);
src/utils/money.js:  const remainder = totalCents - base * count;
src/utils/money.js:  const schedule = [];
src/utils/money.js:  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
src/utils/money.js:    const amount = base + (i < remainder ? 1 : 0);
src/utils/money.js:    const dueDate = new Date(firstDue);
src/utils/money.js:    if (i > 0) dueDate.setMonth(dueDate.getMonth() + i);
src/utils/money.js:    schedule.push({ dueDate, amountCents: amount, status: 'pending' });
src/utils/money.js:  }
src/utils/money.js:  return schedule;
src/utils/money.js:}
src/utils/money.js:
src/utils/money.js:/** Version "montants seuls" (pour HelloAsso terms[]) */
src/utils/money.js:export function splitInstallmentAmounts(totalCents, count) {
src/utils/money.js:  if (![1,2,3].includes(count)) throw new Error('count must be 1|2|3');
src/utils/money.js:  const base = Math.floor(totalCents / count);
src/utils/money.js:  const remainder = totalCents - base * count;
src/utils/money.js:  return Array.from({ length: count }, (_, i) => base + (i < remainder ? 1 : 0));
src/utils/money.js:}
src/utils/money.js:

src/views/renew/renew.js:function buildPricesIndex(prices) {
src/views/renew/renew.js:  const idx = new Map(); // zoneKey -> (tariffCode -> priceCents)
src/views/renew/renew.js:  for (const p of prices) {
src/views/renew/renew.js:    const z = p.zoneKey; const t = String(p.tariffCode||'').toUpperCase();
src/views/renew/renew.js:    if (!idx.has(z)) idx.set(z, new Map());
src/views/renew/renew.js:    idx.get(z).set(t, Number(p.priceCents)||0);
src/views/renew/renew.js:  }
src/views/renew/renew.js:  return idx;
src/views/renew/renew.js:}
src/views/renew/renew.js:
src/views/renew/renew.js:function tariffsForZone(tariffs, pricesIdx, zoneKey) {
src/views/renew/renew.js:  const zMap = pricesIdx.get(zoneKey) || new Map();
src/views/renew/renew.js:  const star = pricesIdx.get('*') || new Map();
src/views/renew/renew.js:  const codes = new Set([...zMap.keys(), ...star.keys()]);
src/views/renew/renew.js:  const list = tariffs.filter(t => codes.has(String(t.code||'').toUpperCase()));
src/views/renew/renew.js:  list.sort((a,b) => (a.code==='NORMAL'?-1:0) + (b.code==='NORMAL'?1:0));
src/views/renew/renew.js:  return list;
src/views/renew/renew.js:}
src/views/renew/renew.js:
src/views/renew/renew.js:const pickDefaultTariff = list => list.find(t => t.code==='NORMAL') || list[0] || null;
src/views/renew/renew.js:
src/views/renew/renew.js:function computeLineAmount(pricesIdx, zoneKey, tariffCode) {
src/views/renew/renew.js:  const t = String(tariffCode||'').toUpperCase();
src/views/renew/renew.js:  const zMap = pricesIdx.get(zoneKey);
src/views/renew/renew.js:  if (zMap && zMap.has(t)) return zMap.get(t);
src/views/renew/renew.js:  const star = pricesIdx.get('*');
src/views/renew/renew.js:  if (star && star.has(t)) return star.get(t);
src/views/renew/renew.js:  return 0;
src/views/renew/renew.js:}

src/views/renew/renew.js:  const code = String(tariffCode || '').normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toUpperCase();
src/views/renew/renew.js:  if (code === 'NORMAL') return { needsJustif:false, justifLabel:'', infoLabel:'', infoPlaceholder:'' };
src/views/renew/renew.js:  if (code.includes('ETU')) {
src/views/renew/renew.js:    return { needsJustif:true,  justifLabel:'Numéro d’étudiant', infoLabel:'Information (optionnel)', infoPlaceholder:'Présentez le justificatif avec votre billet' };
src/views/renew/renew.js:  }
src/views/renew/renew.js:  if (code.includes('LICEN') || code.includes('FFHG') || code === 'LIC') {
src/views/renew/renew.js:    return { needsJustif:true,  justifLabel:'Numéro de licence', infoLabel:'Information (optionnel)', infoPlaceholder:'Présentez le justificatif avec votre billet' };
src/views/renew/renew.js:  }
src/views/renew/renew.js:  return { needsJustif:false, justifLabel:'', infoLabel:'Information (optionnel)', infoPlaceholder:'Présentez le justificatif avec votre billet' };
src/views/renew/renew.js:}

src/views/renew/renew.js:  let scale = 1, tx = 0, ty = 0;
src/views/renew/renew.js:  let dragging = false, last = { x:0, y:0 };
src/views/renew/renew.js:
src/views/renew/renew.js:  function applyTransform() { host.setAttribute('transform', `translate(${tx},${ty}) scale(${scale})`); }
src/views/renew/renew.js:
src/views/renew/renew.js:  function svgPoint(evt) {
src/views/renew/renew.js:    const pt = svg.createSVGPoint();
src/views/renew/renew.js:    pt.x = evt.clientX; pt.y = evt.clientY;
src/views/renew/renew.js:    const m = svg.getScreenCTM().inverse();
src/views/renew/renew.js:    return pt.matrixTransform(m);
src/views/renew/renew.js:  }
src/views/renew/renew.js:
src/views/renew/renew.js:  function onWheel(evt) {
src/views/renew/renew.js:    evt.preventDefault();
src/views/renew/renew.js:    const delta = -(evt.deltaY || evt.wheelDelta || 0);
src/views/renew/renew.js:    const factor = Math.exp(delta * 0.0015);
src/views/renew/renew.js:    const p = svgPoint(evt);
src/views/renew/renew.js:    const newScale = Math.min(10, Math.max(0.25, scale * factor));
src/views/renew/renew.js:    const k = newScale / scale;
src/views/renew/renew.js:    tx = p.x - (p.x - tx) * k;
src/views/renew/renew.js:    ty = p.y - (p.y - ty) * k;
src/views/renew/renew.js:    scale = newScale;
src/views/renew/renew.js:    applyTransform();
src/views/renew/renew.js:  }
src/views/renew/renew.js:
src/views/renew/renew.js:  function onMouseDown(evt) {
src/views/renew/renew.js:    dragging = true;
src/views/renew/renew.js:    $('#venuePlan').classList.add('grabbing');
src/views/renew/renew.js:    last = svgPoint(evt);
src/views/renew/renew.js:  }
src/views/renew/renew.js:  function onMouseMove(evt) {
src/views/renew/renew.js:    if (!dragging) return;
src/views/renew/renew.js:    const p = svgPoint(evt);
src/views/renew/renew.js:    tx += (p.x - last.x);
src/views/renew/renew.js:    ty += (p.y - last.y);
src/views/renew/renew.js:    last = p;
src/views/renew/renew.js:    applyTransform();
src/views/renew/renew.js:  }
src/views/renew/renew.js:  function onMouseUp() {
src/views/renew/renew.js:    dragging = false;
src/views/renew/renew.js:    $('#venuePlan').classList.remove('grabbing');
src/views/renew/renew.js:  }
src/views/renew/renew.js:
src/views/renew/renew.js:  svg.addEventListener('wheel', onWheel, { passive:false });
src/views/renew/renew.js:  svg.addEventListener('mousedown', onMouseDown);
src/views/renew/renew.js:  svg.addEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove);
src/views/renew/renew.js:  svg.addEventListener('mouseup', onMouseUp);
src/views/renew/renew.js:  svg.addEventListener('mouseleave', onMouseUp);

src/views/public/public.js:  // util: badge "P" pour provisionné (dans l'espace SVG)
src/views/public/public.js:  function addBadgeFor(elem, label='P') {
src/views/public/public.js:    const svg = map; // parent <svg>
src/views/public/public.js:    const bb = elem.getBBox();
src/views/public/public.js:    const r = Math.max(6, Math.min(bb.width, bb.height) * 0.20);
src/views/public/public.js:    const cx = bb.x + bb.width - r - 1;
src/views/public/public.js:    const cy = bb.y + r + 1;
src/views/public/public.js:
src/views/public/public.js:    const g = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg','g');
src/views/public/public.js:    g.setAttribute('class','badge');
src/views/public/public.js:
src/views/public/public.js:    const c = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg','circle');
src/views/public/public.js:    c.setAttribute('cx', cx);
src/views/public/public.js:    c.setAttribute('cy', cy);
src/views/public/public.js:    c.setAttribute('r', r);
src/views/public/public.js:
src/views/public/public.js:    const t = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg','text');
src/views/public/public.js:    t.setAttribute('x', cx);
src/views/public/public.js:    t.setAttribute('y', cy);
src/views/public/public.js:    t.textContent = label;
src/views/public/public.js:
src/views/public/public.js:    g.appendChild(c); g.appendChild(t);
src/views/public/public.js:    // on insère juste après l’élément pour rester au-dessus
src/views/public/public.js:    elem.parentNode.insertBefore(g, elem.nextSibling);
src/views/public/public.js:    return g;
src/views/public/public.js:  }

src/views/public/public.js:      const sid = el.getAttribute('data-seat-id');
src/views/public/public.js:      const rec = byId.get(sid);
src/views/public/public.js:      const state = rec ? rec.status : 'available';
src/views/public/public.js:      el.dataset.state = state;
src/views/public/public.js:      el.classList.add('seat');
src/views/public/public.js:

src/views/public/public.js:      else if (state === 'booked')  addBadgeFor(el, 'X');       // optionnel

src/views/public/public.js:  }

src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:  // ---------- Helpers ----------
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:  const $ = sel => document.querySelector(sel);

src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:  function loadAuthHeader() {
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:    return localStorage.getItem('bts_admin_auth_header') || '';
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:  }
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:  function saveAuthHeader(v) {
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:    localStorage.setItem('bts_admin_auth_header', v || '');
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:  }
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:  function makeHeaders() {
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:    const h = { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' };
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:    const raw = loadAuthHeader().trim();
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:    if (!raw) return h;
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:    if (raw.includes(':')) {
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:      const i = raw.indexOf(':');
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:      const k = raw.slice(0, i).trim();
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:      const v = raw.slice(i + 1).trim();
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:      if (k && v) h[k] = v;

src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:  function parseCsv(text) {
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:    const lines = text.replace(/\r/g,'').split('\n').filter(Boolean);
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:    if (!lines.length) return [];
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:    const headers = splitCsvLine(lines[0]).map(h => h.trim());
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:    const rows = [];
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:    for (let i = 1; i < lines.length; i++) {
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:      const cells = splitCsvLine(lines[i]);
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:      const obj = {};
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:      headers.forEach((h, idx) => obj[h] = cells[idx] ?? '');
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:      rows.push(obj);
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:    }
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:    return rows;
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:  }
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:  function splitCsvLine(line) {
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:    const out = [];
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:    let cur = '', q = false;
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:    for (let i=0; i<line.length; i++) {
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:      const c = line[i];
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:      if (q) {
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:        if (c === '"' && line[i+1] === '"') { cur += '"'; i++; }
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:        else if (c === '"') { q = false; }
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:        else cur += c;
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:      } else {
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:        if (c === '"') q = true;
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:        else if (c === ',') { out.push(cur); cur = ''; }
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:        else cur += c;
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:      }
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:    }
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:    out.push(cur);
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:    return out;
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:  }
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:  function parseBool(v, def=true) {
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:    if (v == null || v === '') return def;
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:    const s = String(v).trim().toLowerCase();
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:    if (['1','true','yes','y','on'].includes(s)) return true;
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:    if (['0','false','no','n','off'].includes(s)) return false;
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:    return def;
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:  }
src/public/static/js/admin-pricing.js:

scripts/email/send-renew-invites.js:      } catch (e) {

scripts/email/send-renew-invites.js:      }
scripts/email/send-renew-invites.js:      // Rate limit (évite le throttle Gmail)
scripts/email/send-renew-invites.js:      await delay(1500);

scripts/email/send-renew-invites.js:  }

scripts/admin/upsert-season.js:function parseArgs(argv){
scripts/admin/upsert-season.js:  const [,, seasonCode, ...rest] = argv;
scripts/admin/upsert-season.js:  const opts = { seasonCode, name:null, renewalOpen:null, renewalClose:null, enable:null, disable:null };
scripts/admin/upsert-season.js:  for (const t of rest) {
scripts/admin/upsert-season.js:    let m = /^--name=(.+)$/.exec(t); if (m) { opts.name = m[1]; continue; }
scripts/admin/upsert-season.js:    m = /^--renewal-open=(.+)$/.exec(t); if (m) { opts.renewalOpen = new Date(m[1]); continue; }
scripts/admin/upsert-season.js:    m = /^--renewal-close=(.+)$/.exec(t); if (m) { opts.renewalClose = new Date(m[1]); continue; }
scripts/admin/upsert-season.js:    if (t === '--enable-renewal')  opts.enable = 'renewal';
scripts/admin/upsert-season.js:    if (t === '--disable-renewal') opts.disable = 'renewal';
scripts/admin/upsert-season.js:    if (t === '--enable-public')   opts.enable = 'public';
scripts/admin/upsert-season.js:    if (t === '--disable-public')  opts.disable = 'public';
scripts/admin/upsert-season.js:  }
scripts/admin/upsert-season.js:  return opts;
scripts/admin/upsert-season.js:}
scripts/admin/upsert-season.js:
scripts/admin/upsert-season.js:(async () => {

scripts/admin/upsert-season.js:

scripts/admin/upsert-season.js:  }

scripts/admin/upsert-season.js:
scripts/admin/upsert-season.js:  // ensure phases exists
scripts/admin/upsert-season.js:  const need = (n) => season.phases.find(p => p.name === n) || season.phases.push({ name:n, enabled:true }) && season.phases.find(p => p.name===n);
scripts/admin/upsert-season.js:
scripts/admin/upsert-season.js:  if (renewalOpen || renewalClose) {

scripts/admin/upsert-season.js:    if (renewalOpen)  pr.openAt  = renewalOpen;

scripts/admin/upsert-season.js:  }

scripts/admin/upsert-season.js:

scripts/pricing/import-zone-tariffs.js:function detectDelimiter(line) {
scripts/pricing/import-zone-tariffs.js:  if (explicitDelim === ',' || explicitDelim === ';') return explicitDelim;
scripts/pricing/import-zone-tariffs.js:  let comma = 0, semi = 0, inQ = false;
scripts/pricing/import-zone-tariffs.js:  for (let i=0;i<line.length;i++) {
scripts/pricing/import-zone-tariffs.js:    const ch = line[i];
scripts/pricing/import-zone-tariffs.js:    if (ch === '"') inQ = !inQ;
scripts/pricing/import-zone-tariffs.js:    else if (!inQ) {
scripts/pricing/import-zone-tariffs.js:      if (ch === ',') comma++;
scripts/pricing/import-zone-tariffs.js:      else if (ch === ';') semi++;
scripts/pricing/import-zone-tariffs.js:    }
scripts/pricing/import-zone-tariffs.js:  }
scripts/pricing/import-zone-tariffs.js:  return semi > comma ? ';' : ','; // défaut: ','
scripts/pricing/import-zone-tariffs.js:}

scripts/pricing/import-zone-tariffs.js:function parseCSVLine(line, delim) {
scripts/pricing/import-zone-tariffs.js:  const out = [];
scripts/pricing/import-zone-tariffs.js:  let cur = '';
scripts/pricing/import-zone-tariffs.js:  let inQuotes = false;
scripts/pricing/import-zone-tariffs.js:
scripts/pricing/import-zone-tariffs.js:  for (let i = 0; i < line.length; i++) {
scripts/pricing/import-zone-tariffs.js:    const ch = line[i];
scripts/pricing/import-zone-tariffs.js:
scripts/pricing/import-zone-tariffs.js:    if (inQuotes) {

scripts/pricing/import-zone-tariffs.js:        if (line[i + 1] === '"') { cur += '"'; i++; }
scripts/pricing/import-zone-tariffs.js:        else { inQuotes = false; }
scripts/pricing/import-zone-tariffs.js:      } else {
scripts/pricing/import-zone-tariffs.js:        cur += ch;

scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:function detectDelimiter(line) {
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:  let comma = 0, semi = 0, inQ = false;
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:  for (let i=0;i<line.length;i++) {
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:    const ch = line[i];
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:    if (ch === '"') inQ = !inQ;
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:    else if (!inQ) {
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:      if (ch === ',') comma++;
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:      else if (ch === ';') semi++;
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:    }
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:  }
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:  return semi > comma ? ';' : ','; // défaut: virgule
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:}

scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:function parseCSVLine(line, delim) {
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:  const out = [];
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:  let cur = '';
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:  let inQuotes = false;
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:  for (let i=0; i<line.length; i++) {
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:    const ch = line[i];
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:    if (inQuotes) {
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:      if (ch === '"') {
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:        if (line[i+1] === '"') { cur += '"'; i++; }
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:        else inQuotes = false;
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:      } else {
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:        cur += ch;
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:      }
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:    } else {
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:      if (ch === '"') inQuotes = true;
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:      else if (ch === delim) { out.push(cur); cur = ''; }
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:      else cur += ch;
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:    }
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:  }
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:  out.push(cur);
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:  return out.map(s => s.trim());
scripts/import-subscribers-flat.js:}
